Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
sosa:Observation(?o) ^
onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^
sosa:resultTime(?o, ?t) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?d, ?t) ^
onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^
swrlb:notExists(onto:sendNotification(?n, true)) ->
swrlb:addValue(onto:sendNotification(?n, true)), onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ core:FeatureOfInterest(?tremor) ^ core:FeatureOfInterest(?bradykinesia) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^
onto:MedicalPrescription(?prescription) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?prescription, "Dopamine") ^
sosa:Observation(?obs) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?bradykinesia) ->
onto:Patient(?p) ^ rdf:type(?p, <http://www.case.edu/MDO#ParkinsonDiseasePatient>)

AnnotationProperty: core:hasTimestamp

    Annotations: 
        rdfs:label "dose time"Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?phr) ^
onto:DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt1) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt2) ^
swrlb:subtract(?diff, ?dt2, ?dt1) ^
swrlb:equals(?diff, 14400) ^  # 4 hours in seconds (assuming time is in seconds)
sosa:Observation(?obs) ^
sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?t) ^
swrlb:between(?t, ?dt1, ?dt2) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
<http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^
onto:hasBradykinesia(?p, ?b) ^
swrlb:equals(?b, true) ^
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ->
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?phr) ^
onto:hasAlertingNotification(?phr, ?n)

SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^

onto:hasDosageTime(?d, ?doseTime) ^
swrlb:subtract(?diff, ?time, ?doseTime) ^
swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?diff, 1800) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ->
onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?phr) ^
onto:DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt1) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt2) ^
swrlb:subtract(?diff, ?dt2, ?dt1) ^
swrlb:equals(?diff, 14400) ^  # 4 hours in seconds (assuming time is in seconds)
sosa:Observation(?obs) ^
sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?t) ^
swrlb:between(?t, ?dt1, ?dt2) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
<http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^
onto:hasBradykinesia(?p, ?b) ^
swrlb:equals(?b, true) ^
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ->
onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^

onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ->
onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?phr) ^
onto:DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt1) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt2) ^
swrlb:subtract(?diff, ?dt2, ?dt1) ^
swrlb:equals(?diff, 14400) ^  # 4 hours in seconds (assuming time is in seconds)
sosa:Observation(?obs) ^
sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?t) ^
swrlb:between(?t, ?dt1, ?dt2) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
<http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^
onto:hasBradykinesia(?p, ?b) ^
swrlb:equals(?b, true) ^
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ->
onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

        owl:topDataPropertyRule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ core:FeatureOfInterest(?tremor) ^ core:FeatureOfInterest(?bradykinesia) ^

sosa:Observation(?obs) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?bradykinesia) ->
onto:Patient(?p) ^ rdf:type(?p, <http://www.case.edu/MDO#ParkinsonDiseasePatient>)

    rdfs:label "Wear4PDmoveOnto"@en,
    owl:versionInfo "v1.0.0"

AnnotationProperty: core:hasTimestamp

    Annotations: 
        rdfs:label "dose time"Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?phr) ^
onto:DailyDosagePlan(?dp) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt1) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?dp, ?dt2) ^
swrlb:subtract(?diff, ?dt2, ?dt1) ^
swrlb:equals(?diff, 14400) ^  # 4 hours in seconds (assuming time is in seconds)
sosa:Observation(?obs) ^
sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?t) ^
swrlb:between(?t, ?dt1, ?dt2) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
<http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^
onto:hasBradykinesia(?p, ?b) ^
swrlb:equals(?b, true) ^
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ^

Import: <https://dahcc.idlab.ugent.be/Ontology/SensorsAndWearables/>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^
onto:Patient(?p) ^
sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?p, ?e) ^
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:NotifyNurse(?n) ^
onto:NotifyRelatives(?r) ^
onto:AlertingNotification(?n), onto:AlertingNotification(?r) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^
onto:sendNotification(?r, true) ->
onto:sendNotification(?n, true), onto:sendNotification(?r, true)

Import: <http://www.case.edu/PMDO>
Import: <http://www.w3.org/ns/sosa/>
Import: <https://dahcc.idlab.ugent.be/Ontology/SensorsAndWearables/>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
sosa:Observation(?o) ^
onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^
sosa:resultTime(?o, ?t) ^
onto:hasDosageTime(?d, ?t) ^
onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^
swrlb:notExists(onto:sendNotification(?n, true)) ->
swrlb:addValue(onto:sendNotification(?n, true)), onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

    SubPropertyOf: 
        owl:topDataPropertyRule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ core:FeatureOfInterest(?tremor) ^ core:FeatureOfInterest(?brady) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?prescription) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?prescription, "Dopamine") ^ sosa:Observation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasResult(?obs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:hasResult(?obs, ?brady) -> onto:Patient(?p) ^ rdf:type(?p, <http://www.case.edu/MDO#ParkinsonDiseasePatient>)

        rdfs:label "dose time"Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?event) ^
sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?patient, ?event) ^
onto:PD_Patient(?patient) ^
onto:NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^
onto:sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^
onto:NotifyRelatives(?relativesNotification) ^
onto:sendNotification(?relativesNotification, true) ->
onto:sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^
onto:sendNotification(?relativesNotification, true)

  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
<http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^

core:isMeasuredByDevice(?o, ?sw) ^
onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^
onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^
core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^
onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?f) ^
sosa:Observation(?f) ^
sosa:resultTime(?f, ?t) ^
sosa:resultTime(?o, ?t) ^
-> onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Import: <https://dahcc.idlab.ugent.be/Ontology/SensorsAndWearables/>Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:isPerformedBy(?e, ?p) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:isPerformedBy(?n, ?p)

  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?event) ^
sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?patient, ?event) ^
onto:PD_Patient(?patient) ^
onto:NotifyNurse(?nurseNotification) ^
onto:sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^
onto:NotifyRelatives(?relativesNotification) ^
onto:sendNotification(?relativesNotification, true) ->
onto:sendNotification(?nurseNotification, true) ^
onto:sendNotification(?relativesNotification, true)

    Annotations: 
        rdfs:label "dose time"Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^
onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^
<http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^
onto:hasBradykinesia(?p, ?b) ^
onto:WalkingActivity(?w) ^
core:isMeasuredByDevice(?w, ?sw) ^
onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^
onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?f) ^
sosa:observedBy(?f, ?sw) ^
sosa:resultTime(?f, ?time) ^
swrlb:greaterThan(swrlb:length(?time), 0) ->
onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) ^
onto:sendNotification(?n, true)
